- "If the keys of the Kingdom have been committed to my hands, who shall open out the mysteries thereof?" - [Return of a prodigal](volume-04/chapter-09.md) — Ch. 9
- "…Priesthoods, yet the Melchisedek Priesthood comprehends the Aaronic or Levitical Priesthood, and is the grand head, and holds the highest authority which pertains to the Priesthood, and the keys of the Kingdom of God in all ages of the world to the latest posterity on the earth, and is the channel through which all knowledge, doctrine, the plan of salvation, and every important matter is revealed from heaven." - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "Adam holds the keys of the dispensation of the fullness of times; i.e., the dispensation of all the times have been and will be revealed through him from the beginning to Christ, and from Christ to the end of all the dispensations that…" - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "Why he magnified the Priesthood which was conferred upon him, and died a righteous man, and therefore has become an angel of God by receiving his body from the dead, holding still the keys of his dispensation; and was sent down from heaven unto" - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "There are many things which belong to the powers of the Priesthood and the keys thereof, that have been kept hid from before the foundation of the world; they are hid from the wise and prudent to be revealed in the last times." - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "The next great, grand Patriarch [after Enoch] who held the keys of the Priesthood was Lamech.'And Lamech lived one hundred and eighty-two years and begat a son, and he called his name Noah, saying, this same shall comfort us concerning our work and the toil of our hands because of…" - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "Thus we behold the keys of this Priesthood consisted in obtaining the voice of Jehovah that He talked with him--Noah--in a familiar and friendly manner, that He continued to him the keys, the covenants, the power and the glory, with which he…" - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "Elijah was the last Prophet that held the keys of the Priesthood, and who will, before the last dispensation, restore the authority and deliver the keys of the Priesthood, in order that all the ordinances may be attended to in righteousness." - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "Because he holds the keys of the authority to administer in all the ordinances of the Priesthood; and without the authority is given, the ordinances could not be administered in righteousness." - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "For therein are the keys of the holy Priesthood, ordained that you may receive honor and glory." - [Revelation of January 1841](volume-04/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "…that my servant Hyrum may take the office of Priesthood and Patriarch which was appointed unto him by his father, by blessing and also by right, That from henceforth he shall hold the keys of the Patriarchal blessings upon the heads of all my people," - [Revelation of January 1841](volume-04/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "That he may act in concert also with my servant Joseph, and that he shall receive counsel from my servant Joseph, who shall show unto him the keys whereby he may ask and receive, and be crowned with the same blessing, and glory, and honor, and Priesthood, and gifts of the Priesthood, that once were put upon him that was my servant Oliver Cowdery;" - [Revelation of January 1841](volume-04/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "Let my servant William Law also receive the keys by which he may ask and receive blessings; let him be humble before me, and be without guile, and he shall receive of my Spirit, even the Comforter, which shall manifest unto him the truth of all things, and shall give him in the very hour what he shall say." - [Revelation of January 1841](volume-04/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "Verily I say unto you, I now give unto you the officers belonging to my Priesthood, that ye may hold the keys thereof, even the Priesthood which is after the order of Melchisedek, which is after the order of my Only Begotten Son." - [Revelation of January 1841](volume-04/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "Which Twelve hold the keys to open up the authority of my kingdom upon the four corners of the earth, and after that to send my word to every creature." - [Revelation of January 1841](volume-04/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "The above offices I have given unto you, and the keys thereof, for helps and for governments, for the work of the ministry, and the perfecting of my Saints;" - [Revelation of January 1841](volume-04/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "First of all, we would express our joy and thanksgiving to Him who rules, and knows the hearts of men, for the heed and diligence with which the Saints in this county have hearkened to the counsel of those whom God has seen fit to send among them, and who hold the keys of this ministry." - [General conference at Nauvoo, 1841](volume-04/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "'The Lesser, or Aaronic Priesthood, is to hold the keys of the ministering of angels, and to administer in outward ordinances." - [General conference at Nauvoo, 1841](volume-04/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "The power and authority of the higher, or Melchisedek Priesthood, is to hold the keys of all the spiritual blessings of the Church--to have the privilege of receiving the mysteries of the Kingdom of heaven--to have the heavens opened to them--to commune with the general" - [General conference at Nauvoo, 1841](volume-04/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "The power and authority of the higher, or Melchisedek Priesthood, was to hold the keys of all the spiritual blessing of the Church, as Jesus said, 'I give unto thee the keys of the kingdom of heaven--whatsoever thou shalt bind on earth shall be bound in heaven,' &c." - [General conference at Nauvoo, 1841](volume-04/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "…will of God; and in these things they shall not prosper, for they have neglected the House of the Lord, the baptismal font, in this place, wherein their dead may be redeemed, and the key of knowledge that unfolds the dispensation of the fullness of times may be turned, and the mysteries of God be unfolded, upon which their salvation, and the salvation of the world, and the redemption of their dead depends;…" - [Affairs in Kirtland and Nauvoo, 1841](volume-04/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "There are certain key words and signs belonging to the Priesthood which must be observed in order to obtain the blessing." - [The Bennett–Dyer correspondence](volume-04/chapter-32.md) — Ch. 32
- "Because they have not a key to unlock, no rule wherewith to measure, and no criterion whereby they can test it." - [A Mason's view of Nauvoo and Joseph](volume-04/chapter-33.md) — Ch. 33
- "The Apostles in ancient times held the keys of this Priesthood--of the mysteries of the kingdom of God, and consequently were enabled to unlock and unravel all things pertaining" - [A Mason's view of Nauvoo and Joseph](volume-04/chapter-33.md) — Ch. 33
- "He spoke of delivering the keys of the Priesthood to the Church, and said that the faithful members of the Relief Society should receive them in connection with their husbands, that the Saints whose integrity has been tried and proved faithful, might know how to ask the Lord and receive an answer; for according to his prayers, God had appointed him elsewhere." - [The keys of the priesthood and the Nauvoo Temple](volume-04/chapter-35.md) — Ch. 35
- "…appointed for them to honor, and in those faithful men whom God has placed at the head of the Church to lead His people; that we should arm and sustain them with our prayers; for the keys of the kingdom are about to be given to them, that they may be able to detect everything false; as well as to all the Elders who shall prove their integrity in due season." - [The keys of the priesthood and the Nauvoo Temple](volume-04/chapter-35.md) — Ch. 35
- "…of the Priesthood which God has established, through the medium of those appointed to lead, guide and direct the affairs of the Church in this last dispensation: and I now turn the key in your behalf in the name of the Lord, and this Society shall rejoice, and knowledge and intelligence shall flow down from this time henceforth; this is the beginning of better days to the poor and needy, who shall be…" - [The keys of the priesthood and the Nauvoo Temple](volume-04/chapter-35.md) — Ch. 35
- "Sunday, May 1, 1842.--I preached in the grove, on the keys of the kingdom, charity, &c." - [The keys of the priesthood and the Nauvoo Temple](volume-04/chapter-35.md) — Ch. 35
- "The keys are certain signs and words by which false spirits and personages may be detected from true, which cannot be revealed to the Elders till the Temple is completed." - [The keys of the priesthood and the Nauvoo Temple](volume-04/chapter-35.md) — Ch. 35

#### Volume 5

- "…meaning in its narrowest as in its broadest sense, the 'government of God, whether represented by a single individual, an institution or a great and complex organization (p. 256); the keys by which angelic administrations may be known (p. 267); the virtue tue of Blood Atonement (p. 296); the physical nature of God, the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit (p. 323, 325, 326); the earth becoming a Urim and…" - [Volume 5 introduction](volume-05/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "It is to be of the things that shall not pass away, but a means of perpetuating the lives and all their purifying, and uplifting relationships And the power to establish these relationships is in the Priesthood of God, the keys of which were restored through Joseph Smith." - [Volume 5 introduction](volume-05/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "The same phraseology is used in section 134 in speaking of Hyrum Smith, who was appointed to hold the keys of the patriarchal blessings upon the heads of God's people; namely, 'Whosoever he blesses shall be blessed, and whosoever he curses shall be cursed; and whatsoever he shall bind on earth shall be bound in heaven; and whatsoever he shall loose on earth shall be loosed in heaven' (v. 93)." - [Volume 5 introduction](volume-05/00-front-matter.md) — Intro

- "…Elders Heber C. Kimball and Willard Richards, instructing them in the principles and order of the Priesthood, attending to washings, anointings, endowments and the communication of keys pertaining to the Aaronic Priesthood, and so on to the highest order of the Melchisedek Priesthood, setting forth the order pertaining to the Ancient of Days, and all those plans and principles by which any one is enabled…" - [Endowment ceremonies introduced](volume-05/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "keys upon them from this hour, if they will not repent and stop their lyings and surmisings, let God curse them, and let their tongues cleave to the roofs of their mouths." - [Endowment ceremonies introduced](volume-05/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "…peculiar evils, weaknesses and difficulties, the object is to make those not so good reform and return to the path of virtue that they may be numbered with the good, and even hold the keys of power, which will influence to virtue and goodness--should chasten and reprove, and keep it all in silence, not even mention them again; then you will be established in power, virtue, and holiness, and the wrath of…" - [Joseph's instructions to the Relief Society](volume-05/chapter-02.md) — Ch. 2
- "thee the keys of the kingdom of heaven; and whatsoever thou shalt bind on earth shall be bound in heaven, and whatsoever thou shalt loose on earth shall be loosed in heaven.'" - [Instructions on baptism for the dead](volume-05/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "Now the great and grand secret of the whole matter, and the summum bonum of the whole subject that is lying before us, consists in obtaining the powers of the holy priesthood; for him to whom these keys are given, there is no difficulty in obtaining a knowledge of facts in relation to the salvation of the children of men, both as well for the dead as for the living." - [Instructions on baptism for the dead](volume-05/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "This, therefore, is the sealing and binding power, and, in one sense of the word, the keys of the kingdom, which consist in the key of knowledge." - [Instructions on baptism for the dead](volume-05/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "…the dispensation of the fullness of times, which dispensation is now beginning to usher in, that a whole and complete, and perfect union, and welding together of dispensations, and keys, and powers, and glories should take place, and be revealed, from the days of Adam even to the present time; and not only this, but those things which never have been revealed from the foundation of the world, but have…" - [Instructions on baptism for the dead](volume-05/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "The voice of Peter, James, and John, in the wilderness between Harmony, Susquehanna county, and Colesville, Broome county, on the Susquehanna river, declaring themselves as possessing the keys of the kingdom and of the dispensation of the fullness of times." - [Correspondence between Emma Smith and Governor Carlin](volume-05/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "…Michael, the Archangel, the voice of Gabriel and of Raphael, and of divers angels from Michael or Adam down to the present time, all declaring their dispensation, their rights, their keys, their honors, their majesty and glory, and the power of their Priesthood; giving line upon line, precept upon precept; here a little, and there a little; giving us consolation by holding forth that which is to come,…" - [Instructions on baptism for the dead](volume-05/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "…that constitutes a prophet; and any man who says he is a teacher or preacher of righteousness, and denies the spirit of prophecy, is a liar, and the truth is not in him; and by this key false teachers and imposters may be detected." - [Joseph at Springfield, Illinois](volume-05/chapter-12.md) — Ch. 12
- "Zacharias was a priest of God, and officiating in the Temple, and John was a priest after his father, and held the keys of the Aaronic Priesthood, and was called of God to preach the Gospel of the kingdom of God." - [Discourse: "The Mission and Greatness of John the Baptist"](volume-05/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "John was a priest after the order of Aaron, and had the keys of that priesthood, and came forth preaching repentance and baptism for the remission of sins, but at the same time cries out, 'There cometh one mightier than I after me, the latchet of whose shoes I am not worthy to…" - [Discourse: "The Mission and Greatness of John the Baptist"](volume-05/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "John, at that time, was the only legal administrator in the affairs of the kingdom there was then on the earth, and holding the keys of power." - [Discourse: "The Mission and Greatness of John the Baptist"](volume-05/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "The son of Zacharias wrested the keys, the kingdom, the power, the glory from the Jews, by the holy anointing and decree of heaven, and these three reasons constitute him the greatest prophet born of a woman." - [Discourse: "The Mission and Greatness of John the Baptist"](volume-05/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "I have a key by which I understand the scriptures." - [Interpretation of scriptures](volume-05/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "Three Grand Keys by which Good or Bad Angels or Spirits may be Known-- Revealed to Joseph the Prophet, at Nauvoo, Illinois, February 9, 1843." - [Keys for recognizing true angelic visitors](volume-05/chapter-14.md) — Ch. 14
- "These are three grand keys whereby you may know whether any administration is from God." - [Keys for recognizing true angelic visitors](volume-05/chapter-14.md) — Ch. 14
- "The new name is the key word." - [Doctrines taught at Ramus](volume-05/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "See Rev. i: 1-3, which is a key to the whole subject: 'The revelation of Jesus Christ, which God gave unto Him, to show unto his servants things which must shortly come to pass; and He sent and signified it by His angel unto His servant John: who bare record of the word of God, and of the testimony of Jesus Christ, and of all things that he saw." - [Doctrines taught at Ramus](volume-05/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "I give this as a key to the elders of Israel." - [Doctrines taught at Ramus](volume-05/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "Now, there is some grand secret here, and keys to unlock the subject." - [Salvation through knowledge](volume-05/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "Now for the secret and grand key, Though they might hear the voice of God and know that Jesus was the Son of God, this would be no evidence that their election and calling was made sure, that they had part with Christ, and were joint heirs with Him." - [Salvation through knowledge](volume-05/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "Then knowledge through our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ is the grand key that unlocks the glories and mysteries of the kingdom of heaven." - [Salvation through knowledge](volume-05/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "They will be damned, for they reject the most glorious principle of the Gospel of Jesus Christ and treat with disdain and trample under foot the key that unlocks the heavens and puts" - [Salvation through knowledge](volume-05/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "1st key: Knowledge is the power of salvation. 2nd key: Make your calling and election sure. 3rd key: It is one thing to be on the mount and hear the excellent voice. &c., &c., and another to hear the voice declare to you, You have a part and lot in that kingdom." - [Making your calling and election sure](volume-05/chapter-21.md) — Ch. 21
- "President Young said to Brother Addison Pratt and his associates: We commit the keys of opening the gospel to the Society Islands to you, when all the Twelve said, Aye." - [Mission to the Society Islands opened](volume-05/chapter-21.md) — Ch. 21
- "Third, John at that time was the only legal administrator holding the keys of power there was on earth." - [Meaning of the word "Mormon"](volume-05/chapter-21.md) — Ch. 21
- "The keys, the kingdom, the power, the glory had departed from the Jews; and John, the son of Zachariah, by the holy anointing and decree of heaven, held the keys of power at that time." - [Meaning of the word "Mormon"](volume-05/chapter-21.md) — Ch. 21
- "A man never has half so much fuss to unlock a door, if he has a key, as though he had not, and had to cut it open with his jack-knife." - [Credentials of the Twelve](volume-05/chapter-22.md) — Ch. 22
- "Any person that had seen the heavens opened knows that there are three personages in the heavens who hold the keys of power, and one presides over all." - [Credentials of the Twelve](volume-05/chapter-22.md) — Ch. 22
- "I say in the name of Jesus Christ, by the authority of the holy priesthood, I this day turn the key that opens the heavens to restrain you no longer from this time forth." - [Habeas corpus before the Nauvoo Municipal Court](volume-05/chapter-24.md) — Ch. 24
- "…earth to hold this power, (and I have appointed unto my servant Joseph to hold this power in the last days, and there is never but one on the earth at a time on whom this power and the keys of this Priesthood are conferred,) are of no efficacy, virtue, or force in and after the resurrection from the dead; for all contracts that are not made unto this end have an end when men are dead." - [Revelation on eternal marriage and plural marriage](volume-05/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "…is my law, and by the new and everlasting covenant, and it is sealed unto them by the Holy Spirit of promise, by him who is anointed, unto whom I have appointed this power and the keys of this Priesthood, and it shall be said unto them, Ye shall come forth in the first resurrection; and if it be after the first resurrection, in the next resurrection; and shall inherit thrones, kingdoms, principalities,…" - [Revelation on eternal marriage and plural marriage](volume-05/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "David's wives and concubines were given unto him of me, by the hand of Nathan my servant, and others of the prophets who had the keys of this power; and in none of these things did he sin against me, save in the case of Uriah and his wife: and therefore he hath fallen from his exaltation and received his portion; and he shall not inherit them out of…" - [Revelation on eternal marriage and plural marriage](volume-05/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "…Holy Priesthood to take her and give her unto him that hath not committed adultery, but hath been faithful; for he shall be made ruler over many; for I have conferred upon you the keys and power of the Priesthood, wherein I restore all things, and make known unto you all things in due time." - [Revelation on eternal marriage and plural marriage](volume-05/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "Verily, if a man be called of my Father, as was Aaron, by mine own voice, and by the voice of Him that sent me, and I have endowed him with the keys of the power of this Priesthood, if he do anything in my name, and according to my law, and by my word, he will not commit sin, and I will justify him; for he shall do the sacrifice which I require at his hands for his…" - [Revelation on eternal marriage and plural marriage](volume-05/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "And again, verily, verily I say unto you, If any man have a wife who holds the keys of this power, and he teaches unto her the law of my Priesthood as pertaining to these things, then shall she believe and administer unto him, or she shall be destroyed, saith the Lord your God, for I will destroy her; for I will magnify my name upon all those who receive and abide in my law." - [Revelation on eternal marriage and plural marriage](volume-05/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "(Salem) had power and authority over that of Abraham, holding the key and the power of endless life." - [Joseph on the life and character of Judge Elias Higbee](volume-05/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28
- "The sacrifice required of Abraham in the offering up of Isaac, shows that if a man would attain to the keys of the kingdom of an endless life; he must sacrifice all things." - [Joseph on the life and character of Judge Elias Higbee](volume-05/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28
- "Those holding the fullness of the Melchizedek Priesthood are kings and priests of the Most High God, holding the keys of power and blessings." - [Joseph on the life and character of Judge Elias Higbee](volume-05/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28
- "Jesus Christ is the heir of this Kingdom--the only begotten of the Father according to the flesh, and holds the keys over all this world." - [Joseph on the life and character of Judge Elias Higbee](volume-05/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28

#### Volume 6

- "Joseph in Egypt, a savior of his father's house and the Egyptians' Joseph [Smith] at this day holds the keys of salvation not only to the Gentiles, but also to the house of Israel." - [An assessment of Joseph as a religious leader](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "He has a prophet, and he says the Church shall give heed to the words of the Prophet, as he is to hold the keys of the kingdom of God in this life and in the world to come." - [An assessment of Joseph as a religious leader](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "Gave instructions to try those persons who were preaching, teaching, or practicing the doctrine of plurality of wives: for, according to the law, I hold the keys of this power in the last days; for there is never but one on earth at a time on whom the power and its keys are conferred; and I have constantly said no man shall have but one wife at a time, unless the Lord directs otherwise." - [Joseph's discourse on the death of James Adams](volume-06/chapter-02.md) — Ch. 2
- "It was the established order of the kingdom of God: the keys of power and knowledge were with them to communicate to the Saints." - [Movements of the Apostles in the East](volume-06/chapter-02.md) — Ch. 2
- "I anointed him to the patriarchal power--to receive the keys of knowledge and power, by revelation to himself." - [Movements of the Apostles in the East](volume-06/chapter-02.md) — Ch. 2
- "Shall I, who hold the keys of the last kingdom, in which is the dispensation of the fullness of all things spoken by the mouths of all the holy Prophets since the world began, under the sealing power of the Melchizedek Priesthood,--shall I stoop…" - [Correspondence between James Arlington Bennett and Joseph](volume-06/chapter-04.md) — Ch. 4
- "The keys are to be delivered, the spirit of Elijah is to come, the Gospel to be established, the Saints of God gathered, Zion built up, and the Saints to come up as saviors on Mount Zion." - [The sealing powers of the priesthood](volume-06/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "In the first place, suffice it to say, I went into the woods to inquire of the Lord, by prayer, His will concerning me, and I saw an angel, and he laid his hands upon my head, and ordained me to a Priest after the order of Aaron, and to hold the keys of this Priesthood, which office was to preach repentance and baptism for the remission of sins, and also to baptize." - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "And when God sends a man into the world to prepare for a greater work, holding the keys of the power of Elias, it was called the" - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "John did not transcend his bounds. but faithfully performed that part belonging to his office; and every portion of the great building should be prepared right and assigned to its proper place; and it is necessary to know who holds the keys of power, and who does not, or we may be likely to be deceived." - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "That person who holds the keys of Elias hath a preparatory work." - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "The spirit, power, and calling of Elijah is, that ye have power to hold the key of the revelation, ordinances, oracles, powers and endowments of the fullness of the Melchizedek Priesthood and of the kingdom of God on the earth; and to receive, obtain, and perform all the ordinances belonging to the…" - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "This is the power of Elijah and the keys of the kingdom of Jehovah." - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "No. Then what you seal on earth, by the keys of Elijah. is sealed in heaven; and this is the power of Elijah, and this is the difference between the spirit and power of Elias and Elijah; for while the spirit of Elias is a forerunner, the power of Elijah is sufficient…" - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "Elias is a forerunner to prepare the way, and the spirit and power of Elijah is to come after, holding the keys of power, building the Temple to the capstone, placing the seals of the Melchizedek Priesthood upon the house of Israel, and making all things ready; then Messiah comes to His Temple, which is last of all." - [Joseph's discourse on Elias, Elijah, and Messiah](volume-06/chapter-10.md) — Ch. 10
- "If I show, verily, that I have the truth of God, and show that ninety-nine out of every hundred professing religious ministers are false teachers, having no authority, while they pretend to hold the keys of God's kingdom on earth, and was to kill them because they are false teachers, it would deluge the whole world with blood." - [The character of God; God an exalted man](volume-06/chapter-14.md) — Ch. 14
- "Now, if Jacob had the keys, you might talk about James through all eternity and never get the keys." - [The King Follett Sermon](volume-06/chapter-14.md) — Ch. 14
- "(Reads from the German.) John says, 'I baptize you with water, but when Jesus comes, who has the power (or keys) He shall administer the baptism of fire and the Holy Ghost.' Great God!" - [Eternal life is to know God and Jesus Christ](volume-06/chapter-14.md) — Ch. 14
- "It must be built in this the central place; for every man who wishes to save his father, mother, brothers, sisters and friends, must go through all the ordinances for each one of them separately, the same as for himself, from baptism to ordination, washings and anointings, and receive all the keys and powers of the Priesthood, the same as for himself." - [The whole of America is Zion](volume-06/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "the keys of knowledge, power and revelations should be revealed to a witness who should hold the testimony to the world." - [Joseph on his prophetic calling and the resurrection](volume-06/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "Just at the time when some men think that I have no right to the keys of the Priesthood--just at that time I have the greatest right." - [Joseph on his prophetic calling and the resurrection](volume-06/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "All the testimony is that the Lord in the last days would commit the keys of the priesthood to a witness over all people." - [Joseph on his prophetic calling and the resurrection](volume-06/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "Prophet and leader of His people--one to whom He has given the keys of heaven and earth, and the power of David, and said to him: Whatever you bind on earth shall be bound in heaven, and the gates of hell shall not" - [John S. Reid's speech: early days with Joseph](volume-06/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "They make it a criminality for a man to have a wife on the earth while he has one in heaven, according to the keys of the Holy Priesthood; and he then read a statement of William Law's from the Expositor, where the truth of God was transformed into a lie concerning this thing." - [Destruction of the "Nauvoo Expositor"](volume-06/chapter-21.md) — Ch. 21

#### Volume 7

- "…way of warning to the Prophet: 'And I have sent forth the fullness of my gospel by the hand of my servant Joseph; and in weakness have I blessed him: and I have given unto him the keys of the mystery of those things which have been sealed. even things which were from the foundation of the world, and the things which shall come from this time until the time of my coming, if he abide in me, and if not,…" - [Volume 7 introduction](volume-07/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "In like manner the Christ designated St. Peter to succeed him in the leadership of the church, by saying: 'I will give unto thee the keys of the kingdom of heaven, and whatsoever thou shalt bind on earth shall be bound in heaven: and whatsoever thou shalt loose on earth shall be loosed in heaven.' Thus did the Christ before his crucifixion appoint his…" - [Volume 7 introduction](volume-07/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "Remember, REMEMBER that the priesthood and the keys of power are held in eternity as well as in time, and, therefore, the servants of God who pass the veil of death are prepared to enter upon a greater and more effectual work, in the speedy accomplishment of the restoration of all things spoken of by his holy prophets." - [Documents, press excerpts, and movements of leading elders](volume-07/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "The Revelator says, 'He that overcometh will I give power over the nations, and he shall rule them with a rod of iron'; I don't know but he may hold the keys of the plagues that are to be poured out in the last days upon this and other nations." - [Movements of the Apostles in the East and at Nauvoo](volume-07/chapter-16.md) — Ch. 16
- "When God sends a man to do a work, all the devils in hell cannot kill him until he gets through his work; so with Joseph, he prepared all things, gave the keys to men on the earth, and said, 'I may soon be taken from you','" - [Movements of the Apostles in the East and at Nauvoo](volume-07/chapter-16.md) — Ch. 16
- "I have the keys and the means of obtaining the mind of God on the subject." - [Preliminary meetings on the presidency of the Church](volume-07/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "Joseph conferred upon our heads all the keys and powers belonging to the Apostleship which he himself held before he was taken away, and no man or set of men can get between Joseph and the Twelve in this world or in the world to come." - [Preliminary meetings on the presidency of the Church](volume-07/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "…the Twelve, as Apostles of Jesus Christ unto this generation---Apostles whom God has called by revelation through the Prophet Joseph, who are ordained and anointed to bear off the keys of the kingdom of God in all the world." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "If the people want President Rigdon to lead them they may have him; but I say unto you that the Quorum of the Twelve have the keys of the kingdom of God in all the world." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "Here is Heber and the rest of the Twelve, an independent body who have the keys of the priesthood---the keys of the kingdom of God to deliver to all the world: this is true, so help me God." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "where the keys of the kingdom are, and where they will eternally be." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "Or do you want to clip the power of the priesthood, and let those who have the keys of the priesthood go and build up the kingdom in all the world, wherever the people will hear them?" - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "Because Joseph was their file leader, and he has committed into their hands the keys of the kingdom in this last dispensation, for all the world; don't put a thread between the priesthood and God." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "You cannot appoint a prophet; but if you let the Twelve remain and act in their place, the keys of the kingdom are with them and they can manage the affairs of the church and direct all things aright." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "This is the power that turns the key to bestow salvation through all the land, in the way that Joseph commenced it, the first one called to do the same in all the world." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "If the church want the Twelve to stand as the head, the First Presidency of the Church, and at the head of this kingdom in all the world, stand next to Joseph, walk up into their calling, and hold the keys of this kingdom, every man, every women, every quorum is now put in order, and you are now the sole controllers of it." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "If all the quorums of the church were slain, except the high priests, they would rise up with the keys of the kingdom, and have the powers of the priesthood upon them, and build up the kingdom, and the devil cannot help himself." - [Succession: the Twelve sustained, with Brigham Young at their head](volume-07/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "You are now without a prophet present with you in the flesh to guide you; but you are not without Apostles, who hold the keys of power to seal on earth that which shall be sealed in heaven, and to preside over all the affairs of the church in all the world; being still under the direction of the same God. and being dictated by the same spirit,…" - [Church activity resumes after the succession](volume-07/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "This church may have prophets many, and apostles many, but they are all to stand in due time in their proper organization, under the direction of those who hold the keys." - [Church activity resumes after the succession](volume-07/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "I will give you the key." - [Church activity resumes after the succession](volume-07/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "I give this as a key." - [Church activity resumes after the succession](volume-07/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "Elders Orson Hyde and Parley P. Pratt bore testimony that Joseph the Prophet had ordained, anointed and appointed the Twelve to lead the church and had given them the keys of the kingdom for that purpose." - [Church activity resumes after the succession](volume-07/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "Yes, brethren, we verily know and bear testimony, that a cloud of blessing and of endowment, and of the keys of the fullness of the priesthood, and of things pertaining to eternal life, is hanging over us, and ready to burst upon us or upon as many as live worthy of it, so soon as there is a place found on earth to receive it." - [Epistle of the Twelve: moral and spiritual guidance](volume-07/chapter-22.md) — Ch. 22
- "I will take some of my younger brethren who have received the gospel, they have been ordained an elder to hold the keys, etc." - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "'Are the keys of the kingdom taken from Joseph?" - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "He that believes in Jesus as Joseph did, they will never die They may lay down their lives, but they still hold the keys." - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "Years and years before he had the right of holding the keys of the Aaronic priesthood, he was a Prophet, even before he was baptized." - [Duties of the priesthood explained](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "The Bible says God hath set in the church, first Apostles, then comes Prophets, afterwards, because the keys and power of the Apostleship are greater than that of the Prophets." - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "Sidney Rigdon cannot hold the keys without Joseph, if he had held the keys with Joseph and been faithful he would have been with us." - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "If the Twelve do not apostatize they carry the keys of this kingdom wherever they go.'" - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "…name of the Lord Jesus Christ of Nazareth, we lay our hands upon your head to ordain you to the office of Patriarch in this last Church of Jesus Christ, and we bestow upon you the keys and power, and the right and authority of blessing as a patriarch in the Church of Jesus Christ---and we say unto thee, thou shalt be a father to many." - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "We seal upon you the powers we have mentioned with the keys thereof upon your head in the name of Jesus Christ." - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "If an elder wants to go to preaching let him go into the seventies, You are all apostles to the nations to carry the gospel; and when we send you to build up the kingdom, we will give you the keys, and power and authority." - [October 1844 conference: the Church set in order](volume-07/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "…the Book of Doctrine and Covenants and Book of Mormon, they had renounced the pretensions of Sidney Rigdon as being false and felt satisfied that the Quorum of the Twelve held the keys of the kingdom." - [Progress on the Nauvoo Temple](volume-07/chapter-25.md) — Ch. 25
- "…shall go forth and open the door of salvation to the nations and kingdoms of the earth; even thy servants, the seventies, upon whom the burden of thy kingdom does rest, and to whom the keys of the same shall be committed from time to time." - [The great conference of the Seventies](volume-07/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "Of noble seed---of heavenly birth, He came to bless the sons of earth; With keys by the Almighty given, He opened the full rich stores of heaven, O'er the world that was wrapt in sable night Like the sun he spread his golden light." - [The great conference of the Seventies](volume-07/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "…apart to accomplish the great and mighty work of the last days, which was spoken of by the prophets of old. 'No other people,' said he, 'can possibly do this work, for unto us the keys of this last dispensation with the power of the priesthood is given; consequently there is no people under the whole heaven that sustain the same relationship with God, as we do." - [The great conference of the Seventies](volume-07/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "We labored long and faithfully to persuade Brother Emmett to hearken to the counsel of his friends to whom were committed the power, authority and keys for the salvation of Zion and the redemption of her children together with the keys of endowment for the lifting up and exaltation of the heirs of promise---the remnant of Joseph---but our counsels, our persuasions,…" - [Plea for the return of James Emmett's company](volume-07/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28
- "Do you wish to enter into its sacred courts and receive your washings and anointings, and the keys of knowledge and power?" - [Continued progress in Nauvoo, Europe, and the United States](volume-07/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28
- "Every man that gets his endowments, whether he is a high priest or seventy may go into any part of the world and build up the kingdom if he has the keys." - [Parts of the Nauvoo Temple dedicated; endowments given](volume-07/chapter-37.md) — Ch. 37
- "While Elijah revealed the keys of the priesthood pertaining to this ordinance in the Kirtland Temple in 1836--April 3---the Prophet Joseph did not begin teaching baptism for the dead until in October, 1840 (See letter of President Smith to the 'Traveling High Council'--the Twelve Apostles, then in England--this History, vol. iv, p. 231)." - [The First Presidency reorganized: Brigham Young, Heber C. Kimball, Willard Richards](volume-07/chapter-41.md) — Ch. 41
- "Very serious charges are made against Elder Rigdon for insubordination, for claiming to hold keys and authority above any man or set of men in the church. even superior authority and keys thereof than those held by the Twelve; and likewise he had ordained men to positions---places and offices not recognized as properly belonging to the church." - [Return to Winter Quarters](volume-07/chapter-41.md) — Ch. 41
- "This remark is a bit confusing because as a matter of fact Joseph Smith did not receive the Apostleship before he was baptized, for he was baptized on the visit of John the Baptist who restored the keys of the Aaronic priesthood May 15. 1829; and the restoration of the Apostleship came to him and Oliver Cowdery somewhat later, most like:y sometime in June, 1829." - [Return to Winter Quarters](volume-07/chapter-41.md) — Ch. 41

# Mysteries

#### Volume 1

- "This greater Priesthood administereth the Gospel and holdeth the key of the mysteries of the kingdom, even the key of the knowledge of God." - [Volume 1 introduction](volume-01/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "…writings of the Apostolic Fathers indicates not only a deterioration in the character of the teachers in the Church and what is taught, but more especially indicates the progress of the 'mystery of iniquity' which was at work subverting the Christian religion and destroying the Church of Christ." - [Volume 1 introduction](volume-01/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "After six years of hot disputation and frequent appeals by the contestants to the emperor, the council of Nicea was assembled and the mysteries of the Christian faith submitted to public debate, a portion of the time, at least, in the presence of the emperor, who, to some extent, seemed to exercise the functions of president over the assembly." - [Volume 1 introduction](volume-01/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "Moreover Paul declared in this very prophecy I am about to quote that the forces which would ultimately bring to pass this universal apostasy from the Christian religion--'the mystery of iniquity--' was already at work even in his day." - [Volume 1 introduction](volume-01/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "For the mystery of iniquity doth already work: only he who now letteth (hindereth) will let (hinder), until he be taken out of the way." - [Volume 1 introduction](volume-01/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "Therefore, I will unfold unto them this great mystery;" - [Joseph receives the Nephite plates](volume-01/chapter-03.md) — Ch. 3
- "Seek not for riches but for wisdom, and behold, the mysteries of God shall be unfolded unto you, and then shall you be made rich." - [Translation of the plates continues](volume-01/chapter-04.md) — Ch. 4
- "And if thou wilt inquire, thou shalt know mysteries which are great and marvelous; therefore thou shalt exercise thy gift, that thou mayest find out mysteries, that thou mayest bring many to the knowledge of the truth, yea, convince them of the error of their ways." - [Translation of the plates continues](volume-01/chapter-04.md) — Ch. 4
- "Ask that you may know the mysteries of God, and that you may translate and receive knowledge from all those ancient records which have been hid up, that are sacred; and according to your faith shall it be done unto you." - [Translation of the plates continues](volume-01/chapter-04.md) — Ch. 4
- "Seek not for riches but for wisdom; and, behold, the mysteries of God shall be unfolded unto you, and then shall you be made rich." - [Restoration of the Aaronic Priesthood](volume-01/chapter-05.md) — Ch. 5
- "Wherefore, I will explain unto you this mystery, for it is meet unto you to know even as mine apostles." - [The Book of Mormon published](volume-01/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "For, behold, the mystery of godliness, how great is it!" - [The Book of Mormon published](volume-01/chapter-08.md) — Ch. 8
- "For I have given him the keys of the mysteries, and the revelations which are sealed until I shall appoint unto them another in his stead." - [Further light on the sacrament](volume-01/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "And I have given unto him the keys of the mystery of those things which have been sealed, even things which were from the foundation of the world, and the things which shall come from this time until the time of my coming, if he abide in me, and if not, another will I plant in his stead." - [Lost books of ancient scripture](volume-01/chapter-12.md) — Ch. 12
- "And now I show unto you a mystery, a thing which is had in secret chambers, to bring to pass even your destruction in process of time, and ye knew it not;" - [Doctrinal development (1831)](volume-01/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "If thou shalt ask, thou shalt receive revelation upon revelation, knowledge upon knowledge, that thou mayest know the mysteries and peaceable things--that which bringeth joy, that which bringeth life eternal." - [Doctrinal development (1831)](volume-01/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "Behold, thou shalt observe all these things, and great shall be thy reward; for unto you it is given to know the mysteries of the kingdom, but unto the world it is not given to know them." - [Doctrinal development (1831)](volume-01/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "And again, I say unto you, that if ye desire the mysteries of the kingdom, provide for him food and raiment, and whatsoever thing he needeth to accomplish the work wherewith I have commanded him;" - [Doctrinal development (1831)](volume-01/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "But unto him that keepeth my commandments I will give the mysteries of my kingdom, and the same shall be in him a well of living water, springing up unto everlasting life." - [The founding of Zion](volume-01/chapter-16.md) — Ch. 16
- "And the keys of the mysteries of the kingdom shall not be taken" - [The founding of Zion](volume-01/chapter-16.md) — Ch. 16
- "showing that the keys of the mysteries of the kingdom of our Savior are again entrusted to man; and the riches of eternity within the compass of those who are willing to live by every word that proceedeth out of the mouth of God--therefore the conference voted that they prize the revelations to be worth to the Church the riches of the whole earth, speaking temporally." - [The apostasy of Ezra Booth](volume-01/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "Behold, thus saith the Lord unto you my servants Joseph Smith, Jun., and Sidney Rigdon, that the time has verily come that it is necessary and expedient in me that you should open your mouths in proclaiming my gospel, the things of the kingdom, expounding the mysteries thereof out of the scriptures, according to that portion of Spirit and power which shall be given unto you, even as I will." - [The Amherst conference](volume-01/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "And to them will I reveal all mysteries, yea, all the hidden mysteries of my kingdom from days of old, and for ages to come, will I make known unto them the good pleasure of my will concerning all things pertaining to my kingdom." - [The Amherst conference](volume-01/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "But great and marvelous are the works of the Lord, and the mysteries of his kingdom which he showed unto us, which surpass all understanding in glory, and in might and in dominion;" - [The vision of the degrees of glory](volume-01/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "A. We are to understand that it contains the revealed will, mysteries, and works of God; the hidden things of his economy concerning this earth during the seven thousand years of its continuance, or its temporal existence." - [The vision of the degrees of glory](volume-01/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "They are they who saw the mysteries of godliness; they saw the flood before it came; they saw angels ascending and descending upon a ladder that reached from earth to heaven; they saw the stone cut out of the mountain, which filled the whole earth,…" - ["The Evening and Morning Star" newspaper](volume-01/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "And this greater priesthood administereth the gospel and holdeth the key of the mysteries of the kingdom, even the key of the knowledge of God." - [Larger views of the doctrine of priesthood revealed](volume-01/chapter-21.md) — Ch. 21
- "Finally, brethren, pray for us, that we may be enabled to do the work whereunto we are called, that you may enjoy the mysteries of God, even a fullness; and may the grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all." - [Spiritual blessings in the Church](volume-01/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "I rejoice that our Heavenly Father hath blessed you greatly, as He also has me, in enabling me to speak the praises of God and the mysteries of the kingdom in other tongues according to promise; and this without throwing me down or wallowing me on the ground, or any thing unbecoming or immoral, also, without any external operation of the system, but…" - [Spiritual blessings in the Church](volume-01/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "And from time to time, as shall be manifested by the Comforter, receive revelations to unfold the mysteries of the kingdom" - [Spiritual blessings in the Church](volume-01/chapter-23.md) — Ch. 23
- "And I will bless him with a multiplicity of blessings, in expounding all scriptures and mysteries to the edification of the school, and of the church in Zion." - [Mob violence in Zion (Jackson County)](volume-01/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28

#### Volume 2

- "All of which passages, however, have been regarded as among the mysteries of the word of God, incomprehensible, dark." - [Volume 2 introduction](volume-02/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "Joseph I lay my hands upon thy head, and pronounce the blessings of thy progenitors upon thee, that thou mayest hold the keys of the mysteries of the kingdom of heaven until the coming of the Lord." - [Organization of the High Council](volume-02/chapter-02.md) — Ch. 2
- "To have the privilege of receiving the mysteries of the kingdom of heaven, to have the heavens opened unto them, to commune with the general assembly and Church of the first-born, and to enjoy the communion and presence of God the Father, and Jesus, the Mediator of Covenant." - [The great revelation on priesthood](volume-02/chapter-14.md) — Ch. 14
- "…of in this interrogation, is a personal pronoun, and refers to the multitude.] He answered and said unto them, [that is unto the disciples,] because it is given unto you to know the mysteries of the Kingdom of Heaven, but to them, [that is, unbelievers,] it is not given; for whosoever hath, to him shall be given, and he shall have more abundance; but whosoever hath not, from him shall be taken away even…" - [Joseph's address to the elders](volume-02/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "Men are in the habit, when the truth is exhibited by the servants of God, of saying, All is mystery; they have spoken in parables, and, therefore, are not to be understood." - [Joseph's address to the elders](volume-02/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "that received seed into the good ground is he that heareth the word, and understandeth it, which also beareth fruit, and bringeth forth, some an hundred fold, some sixty, some thirty.' Thus the Savior Himself explains unto His disciples the parable which He put forth, and left no mystery or darkness upon the minds of those who firmly believe on His words." - [Joseph's address to the elders](volume-02/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "To you, He says, (speaking to His disciples,) it is given to know the mysteries of the Kingdom of God." - [Joseph's address to the elders](volume-02/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "…Savior's explanation of the parable, which is so plain that there is no room left for dubiety upon the mind, notwithstanding the cry of the priests--'parables, parables! figures, figures! mystery, mystery! all is mystery!' But we find no room for doubt here, as the parables were all plainly elucidated." - [Joseph's address to the elders](volume-02/chapter-19.md) — Ch. 19
- "Speaking of the pleasure of his companionship with the Prophet, Elder Pratt also says: 'As we journeyed day after day, and generally lodged together, we had much sweet communion concerning the things of God and the mysteries of His kingdom, and I received many admonitions and instructions which I shall never forget.' (Ibid., p. 117.)" - [The gathering Saints](volume-02/chapter-36.md) — Ch. 36
- "…for verily you know, that 'there 's more joy in heaven, over one sinner that repents, than there is over ninety and nine just persons that need no repentance.' Strive not about the mysteries of the kingdom; cast not your pearls before swine, give not the bread of the children to dogs, lest you and the children should suffer, and you thereby offend your righteous Judge." - [Councils in Zion and Kirtland](volume-02/chapter-36.md) — Ch. 36
- "…that of 'evangelist;' and 4th, that of 'pastor.' The apostles are invested with spiritual prerogatives; they alone can administer the Holy Ghost by laying on of hands; to them the mysteries of God are revealed and unfolded to the church; and they decide on matter' of order and discipline." - [Councils in Zion and Kirtland](volume-02/chapter-36.md) — Ch. 36
- "If we, with our learning and intelligence, could not find out the first principles, which was the case with myself and millions of others, how can we find out the mysteries of the kingdom?" - [The gathering Saints](volume-02/chapter-36.md) — Ch. 36

#### Volume 3

- "Brother Thomas Webster will not abide in the Spirit of the Lord, but will reject the truth, and become the enemy of the people of God, and expose the mysteries that have been committed to him, that a righteous judgment may be executed upon him, unless he speedily repent." - [The work in England, 1838](volume-03/chapter-02.md) — Ch. 2
- "I also gave some instructions in the mysteries of the kingdom of God; such as the history of the planets, Abraham's writings upon the planetary systems, etc." - [Settling affairs at Far West](volume-03/chapter-03.md) — Ch. 3
- "Now, the Apostle Paul says on this wise: 'For I would not brethren, that ye should be ignorant of this mystery, lest ye should be wise in your own conceits; that blindness in part is happened to Israel, until the fullness of the Gentiles be come in." - [Epistle of David W. Patten](volume-03/chapter-05.md) — Ch. 5
- "Why of this mystery, that blindness in part had to Israel." - [Epistle of David W. Patten](volume-03/chapter-05.md) — Ch. 5
- "For Paul says again, in speaking of the dispensation of the fullness of times, 'Having made known unto us the mystery of His will, according to His good pleasure, which He hath purposed in Himself: that in the dispensation of the fullness of times, He might gather together in one all things in Christ, both which are in heaven, and which are on earth; even in Him.'(Ephesians, i: 9)." - [Epistle of David W. Patten](volume-03/chapter-05.md) — Ch. 5
- "…on the Scriptures to prove the assertion: 'If ye have heard of the dispensation of the grace of God, which is given me to you-ward : how that by revelation he made known unto me the mystery; (as I wrote afore in few words).' (Ephesians, iii: 2.) And also, Colossians, i: 25." - [Epistle of David W. Patten](volume-03/chapter-05.md) — Ch. 5
- "…out a great glory that was then hovering over the Church, and would soon burst upon the Saints as a cloud by day, and a pillar of fire by night, and would soon unveil the slumbering mysteries of heaven, which would gladden the hearts and arouse the stupid spirits of the Saints of the latter-day, and fill their hearts with that love which is unspeakable and full of glory, and arm them with power, that…" - [Mob movements around Far West](volume-03/chapter-13.md) — Ch. 13
- "the mysteries of those things that have been kept hid from the foundation of the world until now." - [Joseph's letters from Liberty Jail](volume-03/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "…they will take up their abode with him, and the visions of the heavens will be opened unto him, and the Lord will teach him face to face, and he may have a perfect knowledge of the mysteries of the Kingdom of God; and this is the state and place the ancient Saints arrived at when they had such glorious visions--Isaiah, Ezekiel, John upon the Isle of Patmos, St. Paul in the three heavens, and all the…" - [Doctrinal development (1839)](volume-03/chapter-25.md) — Ch. 25
- "I will give you one of the Keys of the mysteries of the Kingdom." - [Joseph's ministry around Commerce](volume-03/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "We would moreover say, abide by that revelation which says 'Preach nothing but repentance to this generation,' and leave the further mysteries of the kingdom till God shall tell you to preach them, which is not now." - [Epistle of the Twelve to the Church](volume-03/chapter-27.md) — Ch. 27
- "The horns of the beast, the toes of the image, the frogs, and the beast mentioned by John, are not going to save this generation; for if a man does not become acquainted with the first principles of the Gospel, how shall he understand those greater mysteries, which the most wise cannot understand without revelation?" - [Epistle of the Twelve to the Church](volume-03/chapter-27.md) — Ch. 27

#### Volume 4

- "from the beginning to Christ, and from Christ to the end of all the dispensations that are to be revealed. 'Having made known unto us the mystery of His will, according to His good pleasure which He hath purposed in Himself' that in the dispensation of the fullness of times He might gather together in one all things in Christ, both which are in heaven, and…" - [Volume 4 introduction](volume-04/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "You will remember the words of the Savior to His disciples; He says, to you is given to know the mysteries of the kingdom of heaven, but to them that are without, all things are in parables." - [Development of the work in England](volume-04/chapter-06.md) — Ch. 6
- "If the keys of the Kingdom have been committed to my hands, who shall open out the mysteries thereof?" - [Return of a prodigal](volume-04/chapter-09.md) — Ch. 9
- "…have been and will be revealed through him from the beginning to Christ, and from Christ to the end of all the dispensations that are to be revealed.'Having made known unto us the mystery of His will, according to His good pleasure which He hath purposed in Himself: that in the dispensation of the fullness of times He might gather together in one all things in Christ, both which are in heaven, and…" - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "Paul to minister consoling words, and to commit unto him a knowledge of the mysteries of godliness." - [The doctrine of priesthood](volume-04/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "Thou shalt know the doctrine of Jesus Christ, and the mysteries of heaven shall be opened to thy mind." - [Twelfth anniversary of the Church's organization](volume-04/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "The power and authority of the higher, or Melchisedek Priesthood, is to hold the keys of all the spiritual blessings of the Church--to have the privilege of receiving the mysteries of the Kingdom of heaven--to have the heavens opened to them--to commune with the general" - [General conference at Nauvoo, 1841](volume-04/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "They were to have he privilege of knowing the mysteries of the kingdom of heaven." - [Differences between Baptists and Latter-day Saints](volume-04/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "'To you it is given to know the mysteries of the kingdom'--to have the heavens opened unto them--to commune with the general assembly and Church of the First born; and to enjoy the communion and presence of God the Father, and of Jesus the Mediator of the new covenant: Heb. xii: 22, 23, 24." - [General conference at Nauvoo, 1841](volume-04/chapter-18.md) — Ch. 18
- "President Brigham Young addressed the Elders at some length, an the importance of teaching abroad the first principles of the Gospel, leaving the mysteries of the kingdom to be taught among the Saints, also on the propriety of many of the Elders remaining at home, and working on the Lord's House; and that their labors will be as acceptable to the Lord as their going…" - [Doctrinal sermon by Joseph](volume-04/chapter-25.md) — Ch. 25
- "…Lord, the baptismal font, in this place, wherein their dead may be redeemed, and the key of knowledge that unfolds the dispensation of the fullness of times may be turned, and the mysteries of God be unfolded, upon which their salvation, and the salvation of the world, and the redemption of their dead depends; for 'thus saith the Lord,' there shall not be a general assembly for a general conference…" - [Orson Hyde's dedicatory prayer on the Mount of Olives](volume-04/chapter-26.md) — Ch. 26
- "We have been told that some individual or individuals have, under false pretenses, been wishing to palm their wicked and devilish principles upon the authorities of the Church, stating that it was part and parcel of the Gospel which God had revealed, and that it is one of the mysteries which the initiated only are acquainted with." - [The moral law of the Church](volume-04/chapter-27.md) — Ch. 27
- "…founded in falsehood, and is the offspring of the devil; and it is at variance with every principle of righteousness and truth, and will damn all that are connected with it, for all mysteries are only such to the ignorant, and vanish as soon as men have sufficient intelligence to comprehend them; and there are no mysteries connected with godliness and our holy religion, but what are pure, innocent, virtuous,…" - [The moral law of the Church](volume-04/chapter-27.md) — Ch. 27
- "If this [the foregoing practice of thieving] is a mystery, it is the 'mystery of iniquity.' We are at a loss to know who could be vile enough to propagate such base and unfounded statements, and we would say to the Church, beware of such men!" - [The moral law of the Church](volume-04/chapter-27.md) — Ch. 27
- "Or who can drag into daylight and develop the hidden mysteries of the false spirits that so frequently are made manifest among the Latter-day Saints?" - ["Try the Spirits" — Joseph's editorial](volume-04/chapter-33.md) — Ch. 33
- "The Apostles in ancient times held the keys of this Priesthood--of the mysteries of the kingdom of God, and consequently were enabled to unlock and unravel all things pertaining" - ["Try the Spirits" — Joseph's editorial](volume-04/chapter-33.md) — Ch. 33
- "President Smith then read the 2nd verse--'Though I have the gift of prophecy, and understand all mysteries, and all knowledge; and though I have all faith, so that I could remove mountains, and have not charity, I am nothing.' He then said, though a man should become mighty, do great things, overturn mountains, perform…" - [Joseph's address to the Relief Society](volume-04/chapter-35.md) — Ch. 35

#### Volume 5

- "'The power and authority of the Higher or Melchisedek, Priesthood, is to hold the keys of all the spiritual blessings of the Church--to have the privilege of receiving the mysteries of the kingdom of heaven--to have the heavens opened unto them--to commune with the general assembly and church of the first born, and to enjoy the communion and presence of God the Father, and Jesus the Mediator…" - [Volume 5 introduction](volume-05/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "…Alderman G. W. Harris, by several females who testified that John C. Bennett endeavored to seduce them, and accomplished his designs by saying it was right; that it was one of the mysteries of God, which was to be revealed when the people was strong enough in faith to bear such mysteries--that it was perfectly right to have illicit intercourse with females, providing no one knew it but themselves,…" - [Joseph accused of being an accessory to the assault on ex-Governor Boggs](volume-05/chapter-04.md) — Ch. 4
- "Oh ye elders of Israel, harken to my voice; and when you are sent into the world to preach, tell those things you are sent to tell; preach and cry aloud, 'Repent ye, for the kingdom of heaven is at hand; repent and believe the Gospel.' Declare the first principles, and let mysteries alone, lest ye be overthrown." - [Eulogy of Lorenzo D. Barnes](volume-05/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "Then knowledge through our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ is the grand key that unlocks the glories and mysteries of the kingdom of heaven." - [Salvation through knowledge](volume-05/chapter-20.md) — Ch. 20
- "Many men will say, 'I will never forsake you, but will stand by you at all times.' But the moment you teach them some of the mysteries of the kingdom of God that are retained in the heavens and are to be revealed to the children of men when they are prepared for them, they will be the first to stone you and put you to death." - [Discourse: "The Purpose of Gathering"](volume-05/chapter-22.md) — Ch. 22
- "Spoke of the elders spending their time in speaking about mysteries and speculating about things beyond the grave which they did not understand." - [Joseph on the life and character of Judge Elias Higbee](volume-05/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28
- "Advised them to wait till God revealed hidden mysteries to them before they undertook to preach them to the people. * * * * Elder Smith counseled the elders to be wise enough to let such things alone, in other words to tell the good things of the kingdom which we have received and do understand, and wait patiently until the Lord takes off the veil of darkness." - [Joseph on the life and character of Judge Elias Higbee](volume-05/chapter-28.md) — Ch. 28
- "Come to me; here's the mysteries man hath not seen, Here's our Father in heaven, and Mother, the Queen; Here are worlds that have been, and the worlds yet to be, Here's eternity, endless; amen." - [Volume 5 notes](volume-05/99-end-matter.md) — Notes

#### Volume 6

- "As many are desirous of hearing mysteries, I will rehearse a short sermon of mysteries for their edification." - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "preach where the Elders had preached all the mysteries about beasts, heads and horns." - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "They wanted us to preach mysteries." - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "We told them we were not qualified to preach mysteries; but if they would send for Elder Kimball he would preach them." - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "So they sent about forty miles for Elder Kimball, and brought him down, they were so anxious to hear mysteries." - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "He arose and remarked that he understood they had sent for him to come and preach the mysteries to them." - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "I want the attention of all.' When every mind was stretched and eager to learn these great mysteries he said, 'The first mystery I shall present before you is this, 'Look at Elder Amasa Lyman; he needs a pair of pantaloons and a new hat." - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "But it appears you do not see it; consequently I want to open your eyes and reveal to you a great mystery; for an Elder in the Church has need of a hat and a pair of breeches as well as yourselves, and especially when the Saints know he is so much in need of them!' He preached a few more mysteries of the same nature,…" - [Conference of the Twelve in Boston](volume-06/chapter-01.md) — Ch. 1
- "It is necessary that men be put in possession of the knowledge and mysteries of the kingdom of God, in order to sin as far as they wish, that they may go to the highest pitch." - [Joseph's memorial to Congress](volume-06/chapter-11.md) — Ch. 11
- "I can enter into the mysteries; I can enter largely into the eternal worlds; for Jesus said, 'In my Father's house are many mansions; if it were not so, I would have told you." - [Eternal life is to know God and Jesus Christ](volume-06/chapter-14.md) — Ch. 14
- "The Elders are sent into the world to preach faith, repentance, baptism for the remission of sins, and the laying on of hands for the reception of the Holy Ghost and they should let the mysteries alone." - [Instructions to elders called on missions](volume-06/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "The Savior says that to you it is given to know the mysteries of God, but to the world it is not given." - [Instructions to elders called on missions](volume-06/chapter-15.md) — Ch. 15
- "I advise all to go on to perfection, and search deeper and deeper into the mysteries of Godliness." - [Joseph on his prophetic calling and the resurrection](volume-06/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "It has always been my province to dig up hidden mysteries--new things--for my hearers." - [Joseph on his prophetic calling and the resurrection](volume-06/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17
- "The Prophet is in good health and spirits, and unwearied in his anxiety and labors to instruct the Saints in the things of God and the mysteries of the Kingdom of Jesus Christ." - [Joseph on his prophetic calling and the resurrection](volume-06/chapter-17.md) — Ch. 17

#### Volume 7

- "…warning to the Prophet: 'And I have sent forth the fullness of my gospel by the hand of my servant Joseph; and in weakness have I blessed him: and I have given unto him the keys of the mystery of those things which have been sealed. even things which were from the foundation of the world, and the things which shall come from this time until the time of my coming, if he abide in me, and if not, another will…" - [Volume 7 introduction](volume-07/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "So many of the saints entrusted with the sacred mysteries of the House of the Lord!" - [Volume 7 introduction](volume-07/00-front-matter.md) — Intro
- "When abroad, they first preached doctrines somewhat like those of the Campbellites; Sidney Rigdon, the inventor of the system, having once been a Campbellite preacher: and when they had made a favorable impression, they began in far-off allusions to open up their mysteries, and to reveal to their disciples that a perfect 'fullness of the gospel' must be expected." - [Ford's speculation on the future of Mormonism](volume-07/chapter-04.md) — Ch. 4
